HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000989/B002","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1451f7c90093e99fab50","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:2:3\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"104:2:4\",\"root_000989\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Serveti saymak, gelecekteki ihtiyaç ve tehlikelere karşı onu hazır bir savunma donanımı gibi tutma kaygısını da açığa çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001","root_001600/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_150714873537d7ee0b8e","text":"3. **Exploratory closed-hand accumulation becoming fuel.** Alongside fixed `ج م ع B001`, `ج م ع B005` images the clenched hollow of the hand; it is activated backward by the squeezing image of `ه م ز B001` in `104:1` and forward by `و ق د B002` in `104:6`, material consumed as fuel. The packet supplies pressure, gathering, wealth, and a kindled fire, but it never states that the wealth is fuel; that conversion is my inference. The fixed act of gathering can therefore be read materially as a closed-handed compression that stockpiles what will feed, rather than prevent, the consuming mechanism. This reading is exploratory because the fuel identity is structurally suggested rather than grammatically asserted.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B008","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_22539bc847078dd8e073","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:2:2\",\"root_000259\",[\"B008\"]],[\"104:2:3\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Biriktirme, sahibine özgürlük vermek yerine onu kendi malına bağlayan gizli bir kısıtlama biçimine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2aac62278a9aba0a3d29","text":"4. **Restraint latent inside accumulation.** Evidence: `ج م ع B008` (restraint gathering hands to neck), activated by `و ص د B001` and `ع م د B003`. Structural cue: gathered possession moves toward immobilizing enclosure. Assigned role: جمع supplies the hidden shackle-image inside collection. Abductive move: when possession becomes the organizing principle, gathered things also gather the possessor into bonds. Reading change: the wealth is already a restraint before explicit punishment arrives.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000989/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_489a7bfeab4e5498c5ad","text":"2. **Counting as preparation against a coming need.** Evidence: `ع د د B002` (preparing equipment, stores, wealth, arms) alongside `م و ل B001`, activated by `خ ل د B001` in 104:3 and `م د د B004` in 104:9. Structural cue: \"collected and counted it\" is followed immediately by the thought that wealth has made him endure. Assigned role: counting functions as readiness for future vulnerability. Abductive move: he counts because he imagines a stored reserve can answer time, death, or exposure. Reading change: the ayah reads as anxious provisioning, not mere greed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B005","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_564d28490ab98eb205f5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:2:2\",\"root_000259\",[\"B005\"]],[\"104:2:3\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kapalı elle biriktirilen servetin, güvenlik sağlayan stok değil, sonraki ateşi besleyebilecek tüketilir bir birikim olduğu ileri sürülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001","root_000989/B001","root_001457/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5df86a6ef3f63a71a2fa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:2:2\",\"root_000259\",[\"B001\"]],[\"104:2:3\",\"root_001457\",[\"B001\"]],[\"104:2:4\",\"root_000989\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Toplama ve sayma zinciri, sınırlı bir servet toplamını kalıcılık kanıtı gibi gösteren yanlış bir hesap düzeni üretir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001","root_000989/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_604ad3dbbf6049400e7b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:2:2\",\"root_000259\",[\"B001\"]],[\"104:2:4\",\"root_000989\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Toplanıp sayılan parçalar, son ayetlerdeki uzatılmış ve düzenli kapatma yapısının maddi mantığını önceden kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8ea9115f4085d02157a0","text":"1. **Hoarded parts become prison-architecture.** Evidence: `ج م ع B001` (gathering scattered parts into one whole), `م و ل B001` (wealth/property), and `ع د د B001` (enumerating), activated by `ع م د B003` and `م د د B001` in 104:9. Structural cue: gathered and counted units precede extended structural units. Assigned role: gathering/counting turns wealth into an ordered aggregate. Abductive move: what the hoarder assembles for possession can be reassembled by the text as the material logic of confinement. Reading change: the ayah is not only about accumulation; it begins constructing the later prison out of counted units.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a73e89d4ccbc1c0addec","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:2:2\",\"root_000259\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Toplama eylemi, serveti sımsıkı tutan kapalı bir el duruşuna dönüşür; daha sonra toplayanın kendisinin atılması bu tutuşu tersine çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000989/B002","root_001466/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c8d2f3ad88058b4c4245","text":"2. **The hoard as defensive equipment.** Fixed `ع د د B002` supplies preparing equipment, stores, arms, or wealth for a coming need. That latent branch is activated by the abrupt corrective and emphatic passive casting in `104:4`, `ن ب ذ B001`. The packet gives stored wealth followed by an event for which it proves useless; I infer that the counting also functions as rehearsal and provisioning against contingency. `وَعَدَّدَهُ` can thus read as an attempt to make wealth ready as protection, with the next movement exposing the reserve's inability to block reversal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001","root_000318/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d028ff269e1616d58663","text":"1. **Counting as a machine for manufacturing permanence.** Fixed `جَمَعَ` realizes `ج م ع B001` (collecting scattered parts into one whole), fixed `مَالًا` realizes `م و ل B001` (acquired wealth), and fixed `عَدَّدَهُ` realizes `ع د د B001` (enumeration). `104:3` adds `ح س ب B001` (reckoning), `ح س ب B002` (supposition), and `خ ل د B001` (enduring permanence). The packet supplies the exact chain gather, count, reckon, permanence; I infer the causal arrow by which repeated enumeration turns a stock into apparent evidence of endless duration. The ayah therefore reads as the construction of a false predictive model, not only as possession plus a repetitive habit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d2fd34c6e443f38a9823","text":"104:4 reversed the whole ayah: the one who gathered is himself نبذ. 104:8-9 made the strongest later surprise: counted wealth no longer looks inert but architectural, as if enumeration trains the form of sealed, extended confinement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_db425bab832c1e5975e8","text":"`104:3` confirmed that enumeration feeds a mental reckoning, but `104:6`-`104:7` supplied the less expected correction: the trajectory ends in kindling and an inner-kindling heart, not in an external balance sheet. This strengthened the reading of counting as a whole permanence-producing economy. It also left the closed-hand-to-fuel reading explicitly exploratory, since the later ayat provide a fuel slot and inward endpoint but never identify wealth itself as the fuel.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f9c108d9ea12f3584ee6","text":"3. **The clenched hand behind wealth and insult.** Evidence: `ج م ع B005` (clenched hollow of the hand), activated by `ه م ز B001` (pressing/squeezing) and `ن ب ذ B001` (casting from the hand). Structural cue: bodily pressure links speech, gathering, and later throwing. Assigned role: the hand-image turns accumulation into grip.
